    40	 * - **15 minute interval**: Standard for POS offline queue (Square=15min, Toast=15min)
    41	 * - **KEEP policy**: Avoid restarting sync on every login (preserves backoff state)
    42	 *
    43	 * **Why Static Object?**
    44	 * - Payment sync scheduling doesn't hold state
    45	 * - Simple API matching Android best practices (like NotificationManager)
    46	 * - Avoids DI complexity for utility class
    47	 *
    48	 * **World-Class References:**
    49	 * - Square Terminal: OfflineSyncScheduler with 15-min interval
    50	 * - Toast POS: PaymentQueueScheduler with 15-min interval
    51	 * - Stripe Terminal: TransactionSyncScheduler with exponential backoff
    52	 */
    53	object PaymentSyncScheduler {
    54	
    55	    private const val PAYMENT_SYNC_WORK_NAME = "payment_sync_worker"
    56	    private const val PAYMENT_SYNC_INTERVAL_MINUTES = 15L // Standard: Square=15min, Toast=15min
    57	
    58	    /** Nombre único del worker inmediato. Sin esto, cada runNow() creaba uno nuevo. */
    59	    const val IMMEDIATE_WORK_NAME = "payment_sync_now"
    60	
    61	    /**
    62	     * Start payment sync monitoring
    63	     *
    64	     * **When to call:**
    65	     * - After successful login
    66	     * - After app restart (if user already logged in)
    67	     * - After first payment is queued
    68	     *
    69	     * **Behavior:**
    70	     * - KEEPS existing worker if already running (KEEP policy)
    71	     * - Starts after 15 minutes, then repeats every 15 minutes
    72	     * - Runs in background even when app is closed
    73	     *
    74	     * **Constraints:**
    75	     * - Requires network connection (CONNECTED)
    76	     * - No battery/charging constraints (payments must sync ASAP)
    77	     *
    78	     * **Important:** Using KEEP policy prevents restarting worker on every login.
    79	     * This preserves WorkManager's exponential backoff state if worker is retrying.
    80	     *
    81	     * @param context Application or Activity context
    82	     */
    83	    fun start(context: Context) {
    84	        Timber.d("🚀 Starting payment sync scheduler (interval: ${PAYMENT_SYNC_INTERVAL_MINUTES}min)")
    85	
    86	        // Define constraints: Only run when network is available
    87	        val constraints = Constraints.Builder()
    88	            .setRequiredNetworkType(NetworkType.CONNECTED) // Don't run when offline
    89	            .build()
    90	
    91	        // Build periodic work request
    92	        val paymentSyncWorkRequest = PeriodicWorkRequestBuilder<PaymentSyncWorker>(
    93	            repeatInterval = PAYMENT_SYNC_INTERVAL_MINUTES,
    94	            repeatIntervalTimeUnit = TimeUnit.MINUTES
    95	        )
    96	            .setConstraints(constraints)
    97	            .addTag(PAYMENT_SYNC_WORK_NAME)
    98	            .build()
    99	
   100	        // Enqueue work (KEEP policy preserves existing worker)
   101	        WorkManager.getInstance(context).enqueueUniquePeriodicWork(
   102	            PAYMENT_SYNC_WORK_NAME,
   103	            ExistingPeriodicWorkPolicy.KEEP, // Keep existing work if already running
   104	            paymentSyncWorkRequest
   105	        )
   106	
   107	        runNow(context) // Arranque/reingreso: también puede haber sólo aperturas o cierres, sin pagos pendientes.
   108	        Timber.d("✅ Payment sync scheduler started")
   109	    }
   110	
   111	    /** APPEND persiste la siguiente pasada aunque la inmediata actual aún esté RUNNING (KEEP la perdería). */
   112	    fun continuarDespuesDeEstaPasada(context: Context) {
   113	        val request = androidx.work.OneTimeWorkRequestBuilder<PaymentSyncWorker>()
   114	            .setConstraints(Constraints.Builder().setRequiredNetworkType(NetworkType.CONNECTED).build())
   115	            .addTag(PAYMENT_SYNC_WORK_NAME).build()
   116	        WorkManager.getInstance(context).enqueueUniqueWork(IMMEDIATE_WORK_NAME, androidx.work.ExistingWorkPolicy.APPEND_OR_REPLACE, request)
   117	    }
   118	
   119	    /**
   120	     * Stop payment sync monitoring
   121	     *
   122	     * **When to call:**
   123	     * - On logout
   124	     * - When terminal is deactivated
   125	     *
   126	     * **Behavior:**
   127	     * - Cancels all pending and running payment sync workers
   128	     * - Gracefully stops background execution
   129	     *
   130	     * **Important:** This does NOT delete queued payments from Room database.
   131	     * Payments remain in queue and will sync when worker is restarted.
   132	     *
   133	     * @param context Application or Activity context
   134	     */
   135	    fun stop(context: Context) {
   136	        Timber.d("🛑 Stopping payment sync scheduler")
   137	
   138	        WorkManager.getInstance(context).cancelUniqueWork(PAYMENT_SYNC_WORK_NAME)
   139	
   140	        Timber.d("✅ Payment sync scheduler stopped")
   141	    }
   142	
   143	    /**
   144	     * Check if payment sync is currently running
   145	     *
   146	     * **Use cases:**
   147	     * - Debugging: Verify sync started after login
   148	     * - UI indicators: Show "Syncing..." badge on PaymentSuccessScreen
   149	     * - Health checks: Ensure sync running in production
   150	     *
   151	     * @param context Application or Activity context
   152	     * @return true if payment sync worker is enqueued or running
   153	     */
   154	    suspend fun isRunning(context: Context): Boolean {
   155	        val workInfos = WorkManager.getInstance(context)
   156	            .getWorkInfosForUniqueWork(PAYMENT_SYNC_WORK_NAME)
   157	            .get()
   158	
   159	        val isRunning = workInfos.isNotEmpty() && workInfos.any { workInfo ->
   160	            !workInfo.state.isFinished
   161	        }
   162	
   163	        Timber.d("Payment sync running: $isRunning")
   164	        return isRunning
   165	    }
   166	
   167	    /**
   168	     * Force immediate sync execution (for testing and manual retry)
   169	     *
   170	     * **Use cases:**
   171	     * - Debugging: Test sync logic immediately
   172	     * - User action: "Sync Now" button in UI
   173	     * - After queueing payment: Trigger immediate sync attempt
   174	     *
   175	     * WorkManager doesn't guarantee immediate execution,
   176	     * but this requests it to run ASAP (usually within 10 seconds).
   177	     *
   178	     * **Important:** This creates a ONE-TIME work request (doesn't affect periodic worker)
   179	     *
   180	     * @param context Application or Activity context
   181	     */
   182	    fun runNow(context: Context) {
   183	        Timber.d("🔥 Requesting immediate payment sync execution")
   184	
   185	        val constraints = Constraints.Builder()
   186	            .setRequiredNetworkType(NetworkType.CONNECTED)
   187	            .build()
   188	
   189	        val immediateWorkRequest = androidx.work.OneTimeWorkRequestBuilder<PaymentSyncWorker>()
   190	            .setConstraints(constraints)
   191	            .build()
   192	
   193	        // KEEP, no REPLACE: si ya hay un worker drenando la cola queremos que TERMINE.
   194	        // REPLACE lo mataria a media tanda y las filas reclamadas tendrian que esperar
   195	        // al umbral de stale-claim para volver a salir.
   196	        WorkManager.getInstance(context).enqueueUniqueWork(
   197	            IMMEDIATE_WORK_NAME,
   198	            androidx.work.ExistingWorkPolicy.KEEP,
   199	            immediateWorkRequest,
   200	        )
   201	
   202	        Timber.d("✅ Immediate payment sync requested")
   203	    }

   115	        /**
   116	         * Unique worker name for WorkManager.
   117	         * Use this to enqueue/cancel work.
   118	         */
   119	        const val WORKER_NAME = "payment_sync_worker"
   120	
   121	        // 🔴 F-10: NO local MAX_RETRY_ATTEMPTS constant here on purpose. The only caller
   122	        // (the SyncOutcome.Permanent branch of syncPayment()) used to release() a row
   123	        // straight to FAILED by claiming retryCount == MAX_RETRY_ATTEMPTS — replaced by
   124	        // markPermanentlyFailedClaim(), which sets FAILED + permanent=true directly and
   125	        // never touches retry_count. PendingPaymentEntity.MAX_RETRY_ATTEMPTS (10) remains
   126	        // the single source of truth for transient-failure retry accounting (release()'s
   127	        // CASE in PendingPaymentDao).
   128	
   129	        /**
   130	         * Maximum payments to process per worker run.
   131	         * Prevents WorkManager 10-minute timeout when queue is large.
   132	         * Remaining payments will be processed in the next periodic run (15 min).
   133	         */
   134	        private const val MAX_PAYMENTS_PER_RUN = 10
   135	
   136	        /** Tope de reembolsos por corrida, por la misma razón que [MAX_PAYMENTS_PER_RUN]. */
   137	        private const val MAX_REFUNDS_PER_RUN = 10
   138	
   139	        /**
   140	         * Espejo por nombre EXACTO del `code` del servidor (`NotFoundError(..., 'ORDER_NOT_FOUND')`
   141	         * en `payment.tpv.service.ts`, serializado por `src/app.ts`). Un nombre mal escrito NO
   142	         * truena: el respaldo a venta rápida simplemente deja de ocurrir y el cobro queda en
   143	         * revisión manual — el lado seguro, pero hay que enterarse. Por eso vive en una constante
   144	         * y no suelto en la comparación.
   145	         */
   146	        private const val CODIGO_ORDEN_INEXISTENTE = "ORDER_NOT_FOUND"
   147	    }
   148	
   149	    /**
   150	     * Execute payment sync task
   151	     *
   152	     * **Return Values:**
   153	     * - Result.success() → All pending payments processed (even if some failed)
   154	     * - Result.retry() → Worker crashed, WorkManager should retry
   155	     * - Result.failure() → Permanent failure (e.g., terminal not activated)
   156	     *
   157	     * **Retry Policy:**
   158	     * This worker ALWAYS returns Result.success() to continue periodic runs.
   159	     * Each payment gets exactly ONE attempt per call to doWork() (F-9) — the retry
   160	     * cadence across attempts is owned by WorkManager (15-min periodic run + the
   161	     * NetworkType.CONNECTED constraint), never by a loop/delay() in this worker.
   162	     *
   163	     * **Important:** Unlike HeartbeatWorker, this worker processes ALL pending payments
   164	     * in a single run. It doesn't stop at the first failure — `markSyncedChecked` /
   165	     * `releaseClaim` / `markPermanentlyFailedClaim` and the repository writes underneath
   166	     * them (`markSynced`, `release`, `markPermanentlyFailed`) all catch internally and
   167	     * return `0` on error, so one payment's write failing (disk full, DB closed
   168	     * mid-teardown) can't throw out of the `for (payment in batch)` loop
   169	     * and abandon the rest of an already-claimed, already-charged batch (F-9, Fix round 1
   170	     * — `release()` used to be the one write in `PaymentQueueRepositoryImpl` without a
   171	     * try/catch, unlike its siblings).
   172	     */
   173	    override suspend fun doWork(): Result {
   174	        // D7: filas que esta pasada reclamó y todavía no resuelve. Si el worker sale antes
   175	        // (WorkManager lo detiene porque la red «validada» parpadeó al reconectar, o una
   176	        // excepción lo tumba), se sueltan aquí; antes esperaban STALE_CLAIM_MS (15 min).
   177	        var sinTerminar: List<com.jaac.avoqado_tpv.features.payment.domain.model.QueuedPayment> = emptyList()
   178	        return try {
   179	            Timber.d("💾 [Payment Sync] Worker started")
   180	
   181	            var sessionProgress = shiftRepository?.sincronizarAperturasLocales() ?: 0
   182	            val tableProgress = tableSyncCoordinator.replayCurrentVenueBatch()
   183	            shiftRepository?.consultarCriptosPendientes()
   184	            var paymentProgress = 0
   185	
   186	            // Reclamar la tanda: otro worker corriendo en paralelo NO puede tomar estas filas.
   187	            // NonCancellable: si WorkManager cancela justo cuando Room ya confirmó el reclamo, un
   188	            // withContext cancelable tira el resultado y nadie sabría qué filas soltar (Codex r1,
   189	            // hallazgo 1). La cancelación se nota en la siguiente suspensión, con la lista ya guardada.
   190	            val batch = kotlinx.coroutines.withContext(kotlinx.coroutines.NonCancellable) {
   191	                paymentQueueRepository.claimBatch(MAX_PAYMENTS_PER_RUN)
   192	            }
   193	            sinTerminar = batch
   194	
   195	            if (batch.isEmpty()) {
   196	                // 🔴 Ya NO se hace `return` aquí: los reembolsos se drenan abajo aunque no haya
   197	                // cobros pendientes. Con el `return` temprano, una cola con sólo reembolsos no se
   198	                // reproducía nunca.
   199	                Timber.d("✅ [Payment Sync] No hay pagos pendientes por sincronizar")
   200	            } else {
   201	                Timber.i("🔄 [Payment Sync] ${batch.size} pagos reclamados para sincronizar")
   202	
   203	                // Process each payment independently (one failure doesn't block others)
   204	                var successCount = 0
   205	                var failedCount = 0
   206	
   207	                for ((i, payment) in batch.withIndex()) {
   208	                    val success = syncPayment(payment)
   209	                    // Sin suspensión entre el return de syncPayment y esta línea: una
   210	                    // cancelación nunca deja fuera de la lista una fila sin resolver.
   211	                    sinTerminar = batch.subList(i + 1, batch.size)
   212	                    if (success) {
   213	                        successCount++
   214	                        paymentProgress++
   215	                    } else {
   216	                        failedCount++
   217	                    }
   218	                }
   219	
   220	                // Log summary
   221	                Timber.i(
   222	                    "✅ [Payment Sync] Worker completed | " +
   223	                            "success=$successCount | failed=$failedCount | batch=${batch.size}"
   224	                )
   225	            }
   226	
   227	            // 💸 Los reembolsos van DESPUÉS de los cobros: un reembolso apunta al pago original
   228	            // por su id de servidor, así que primero tiene que existir el pago allá.
   229	            drainRefunds()
   230	            sessionProgress += shiftRepository?.sincronizarCierresLocales() ?: 0
   231	            // 📸 Las fotos de comprobante van al final de todo (también después de los cierres de caja): cuelgan de un pago que
   232	            // ya tiene que existir en el servidor, y una subida lenta a Firebase no debe retrasar el dinero.
   233	            val moreProofs = drainProofOfSale()
   234	            if ((tableProgress.resolved > 0 && tableProgress.moreAfterLimit) ||
   235	                (paymentProgress > 0 && batch.size == MAX_PAYMENTS_PER_RUN && paymentQueueRepository.getPendingCount() > 0) ||
   236	                (sessionProgress > 0 && shiftRepository?.haySesionesPorReproducir() == true) ||
   237	                moreProofs) {
   238	                com.jaac.avoqado_tpv.core.util.PaymentSyncScheduler.continuarDespuesDeEstaPasada(applicationContext)
   239	            }
   240	
   241	            // 🔴 Avisar al banner (bug real 2026-08-07): el contador de
   242	            // "pagos pendientes" es un StateFlow de push manual
   243	            // (PaymentQueueStateManager) — el boton de reintentar lo refresca al
   244	            // RESETEAR, pero nadie lo refrescaba cuando la tanda TERMINABA. Los 2
   245	            // pagos atorados sincronizaron (success=2) y el banner siguio diciendo
   246	            // "2 pagos pendientes" hasta reiniciar la app: el cajero reintenta un
   247	            // trabajo ya hecho. Refrescar aqui cierra el ciclo con la verdad de Room.
   248	            // En try/catch propio: un fallo al CONTAR jamas puede tirar el worker —
   249	            // los pagos de esta tanda YA se procesaron.
   250	            // 💸 Se refresca SIEMPRE, también cuando sólo hubo reembolsos (auditoría de Codex F10): un
   251	            // reembolso atorado era invisible hasta intentar cerrar el turno.
   252	            try {
   253	                val pending = paymentQueueRepository.getPendingCount()
   254	                val failed = paymentQueueRepository.getFailedCount()
   255	                val pendingRefunds = refundQueueRepository.getPendingCount()
   256	                val failedRefunds = refundQueueRepository.getFailedCount()
   257	                paymentQueueStateManager.refreshCounts(pending, failed, pendingRefunds, failedRefunds)
   258	            } catch (e: CancellationException) {
   259	                throw e
   260	            } catch (e: Exception) {

    39	class PaymentQueueRepositoryImpl @Inject constructor(
    40	    private val pendingPaymentDao: PendingPaymentDao
    41	) : PaymentQueueRepository {
    42	
    43	    override suspend fun enqueue(payment: QueuedPayment): Result<Unit> = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
    44	        try {
    45	            val entity = payment.toEntity()
    46	            // 🔴 F-9, Fix round 3: NonCancellable — la tarjeta YA se cobró antes de que
    47	            // enqueue() se llame (solo se invoca cuando el registro al backend falló); esta
    48	            // fila es la red de seguridad COMPLETA de ese cobro, no trabajo opcional que se
    49	            // pueda abandonar si la pantalla se cierra a mitad de camino (viewModelScope
    50	            // cancelado al hacer clear() del ViewModel — AngelPayPaymentViewModel
    51	            // .handleRecordFailure(), llamado desde dentro de viewModelScope.launch{} en los
    52	            // 3 sitios que registran un pago). Mismo patrón que los marks post-SDK de
    53	            // PaymentAttemptLedger (NonCancellable + Dispatchers.IO — "survive screen pops and
    54	            // ViewModel clears"), aplicado aquí por la misma razón: proteger dinero que YA se
    55	            // movió, no dato que se pueda perder sin costo.
    56	            //
    57	            // Acotado a LA ESCRITURA sola (no a toda la función): en cuanto este insert
    58	            // compromete la fila en SQLite, el dato ya quedó a salvo pase lo que pase después
    59	            // con esta corrutina — que el `return@withContext` de más abajo o el resto de
    60	            // enqueue() se cancelen luego no deshace un commit ya hecho. El diagnóstico de
    61	            // duplicados que sigue (findByReference, cuando hay choque de índice) es una
    62	            // LECTURA sobre una fila que, de existir, ya quedó a salvo por un intento
    63	            // anterior — no protege dinero nuevo y no necesita el escudo.
    64	            val id = withContext(NonCancellable + Dispatchers.IO) {
    65	                pendingPaymentDao.insert(entity)
    66	            }
    67	
    68	            if (id > 0) {
    69	                Timber.i(
    70	                    "💾 [Payment Queue] Pago encolado | ref=${payment.referenceNumber} | " +
    71	                        "amount=${payment.amount} | queueId=$id",
    72	                )
    73	                return@withContext Result.success(Unit)
    74	            }
    75	
    76	            // rowId 0 = choque con el índice único de reference_number.
    77	            // Solo es benigno si la fila existente sigue viva Y es el MISMO pago:
    78	            // - MISMO venue — el índice es global y pending_payments NO se vacía al
    79	            //   cambiar de venue/logout (a propósito: guarda datos de dinero). Un
    80	            //   terminal reasignado de un venue a otro (patrón operativo real —
    81	            //   promotores de relevo, re-parenting) puede reusar un reference_number
    82	            //   corto/secuencial que OTRO venue aún tiene PENDING localmente.
    83	            //   findByReference ya filtra por venue_id en el SQL, así que una fila de
    84	            //   otro venue es invisible aquí (Fix round 1, finding 1).
    85	            // - MISMA idempotencyKey cuando ambos lados la tienen — mismo venue+reference
    86	            //   con idempotencyKey distinta es un pago DISTINTO, no un doble-submit seguro.
    87	            val existing = pendingPaymentDao.findByReference(payment.referenceNumber, payment.venueId)
    88	            val isCash = payment.toCardDetails().isCash
    89	            val sameIdentity = existing != null && if (isCash) {
    90	                // Un UUID nuevo no puede acreditarse por una fila legacy sin UUID.
    91	                !payment.idempotencyKey.isNullOrBlank() && payment.idempotencyKey == existing.idempotencyKey
    92	            } else {
    93	                payment.idempotencyKey == null || existing.idempotencyKey == null ||
    94	                    payment.idempotencyKey == existing.idempotencyKey
    95	            }
    96	            // El fallback de efectivo no puede acreditar otra venta sólo porque comparte
    97	            // referencia/UUID. Se compara el dinero por valor, no por formato decimal.
    98	            val comparable = if (sameIdentity && entity.shiftMembership == "UNKNOWN" && existing != null) entity.copy(
    99	                shiftMembership = existing.shiftMembership, localShiftSessionId = existing.localShiftSessionId,
   100	                cashDrawerSessionId = existing.cashDrawerSessionId, shiftId = existing.shiftId) else entity
   101	            val sameCashPayload = !payment.toCardDetails().isCash || (existing != null &&
   102	                entity.amount.toBigDecimal().compareTo(existing.amount.toBigDecimal()) == 0 &&
   103	                entity.tip.toBigDecimal().compareTo(existing.tip.toBigDecimal()) == 0 &&
   104	                entity.staffId == existing.staffId && comparable.shiftId == existing.shiftId &&
   105	                comparable.shiftMembership == existing.shiftMembership && comparable.localShiftSessionId == existing.localShiftSessionId &&
   106	                comparable.cashDrawerSessionId == existing.cashDrawerSessionId &&
   107	                entity.orderId == existing.orderId && entity.deviceSerialNumber == existing.deviceSerialNumber &&
   108	                entity.terminalPaymentRequestId == existing.terminalPaymentRequestId &&
   109	                entity.paymentProcessor == existing.paymentProcessor &&
   110	                entity.isPortabilidad == existing.isPortabilidad && entity.serialNumbers == existing.serialNumbers &&
   111	                entity.splitType == existing.splitType && entity.paidProductIds == existing.paidProductIds &&
   112	                entity.equalPartsPartySize == existing.equalPartsPartySize &&
   113	                entity.equalPartsPayedFor == existing.equalPartsPayedFor)
   114	            // Efectivo con la MISMA llave y el mismo dinero ya SUCCESS = esta misma venta, que el worker subió entre el `release`
   115	            // de la pantalla y este encolado (encargo 97042c, Codex r1 #8). Está registrada: no es un error ni pide «Reintentar».
   116	            val yaRegistrada = isCash && sameIdentity && sameCashPayload &&
   117	                existing?.syncStatus == PendingPaymentEntity.SYNC_STATUS_SUCCESS
   118	            val stillQueued = sameIdentity && sameCashPayload &&
   119	                (existing?.syncStatus == PendingPaymentEntity.SYNC_STATUS_PENDING ||
   120	                    existing?.syncStatus == PendingPaymentEntity.SYNC_STATUS_SYNCING) || yaRegistrada
   121	
   122	            if (stillQueued) {
   123	                Timber.w("⚠️ [Payment Queue] Pago ya encolado | ref=${payment.referenceNumber}")
   124	                Result.success(Unit)
   125	            } else {
   126	                // 🔴 Fila SUCCESS/FAILED vieja, de OTRO venue, o de OTRO pago (idempotencyKey
   127	                // distinta) bloquea el índice: este pago NO entró. Reportarlo como fallo es
   128	                // lo que hace que el cajero vea el aviso rojo real ("avisa al supervisor")
   129	                // en vez del falso "EN COLA".
   130	                Timber.e(
   131	                    "❌ [Payment Queue] Encolado BLOQUEADO por fila previa (%s) | ref=%s venue=%s",
   132	                    existing?.syncStatus ?: "de otro venue/pago",
   133	                    payment.referenceNumber,
   134	                    payment.venueId,
   135	                )
   136	                Result.failure(
   137	                    IllegalStateException(
   138	                        "El pago no pudo encolarse: ya existe una fila ${existing?.syncStatus ?: "de otro venue/pago"} " +
   139	                            "con reference ${payment.referenceNumber}",
   140	                    ),
   141	                )
   142	            }
   143	        } catch (e: Exception) {
   144	            Timber.e(e, "❌ [Payment Queue] Failed to enqueue payment")
   145	            Result.failure(e)
   146	        }
   147	    }
   148	
   149	    override suspend fun enqueueAndClaim(payment: QueuedPayment): Result<QueuedPayment> =
   150	        withContext(NonCancellable + Dispatchers.IO) {
   151	            try {
   152	                require(!payment.idempotencyKey.isNullOrBlank()) { "El efectivo necesita su llave de reintento" }
   153	                val token = java.util.UUID.randomUUID().toString()
   154	                val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
   155	                val entity = payment.toEntity().copy(
   156	                    syncStatus = PendingPaymentEntity.SYNC_STATUS_SYNCING,
   157	                    claimToken = token,
   158	                    claimedAt = now,
   159	                )
   160	                val id = pendingPaymentDao.insert(entity)
   161	                check(id > 0) { "El pago ya está guardado; no se enviará otra vez desde esta pantalla" }
   162	                Result.success(entity.copy(id = id).toQueuedPayment())
   163	            } catch (e: Exception) {
   164	                Result.failure(e)
   165	            }
   166	        }
   167	
   168	    override suspend fun getAllPending(): List<QueuedPayment> = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
   169	        try {
   170	            val entities = pendingPaymentDao.getAllPending()
   171	            Timber.d("📋 [Payment Queue] Fetched ${entities.size} pending payments")
   172	            entities.map { it.toQueuedPayment() }
   173	        } catch (e: Exception) {
   174	            Timber.e(e, "❌ [Payment Queue] Failed to fetch pending payments")
   175	            emptyList()
   176	        }
   177	    }
   178	
   179	    override suspend fun markSynced(queueId: Long, token: String): Int = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
   180	        try {
   181	            val affected = pendingPaymentDao.markSynced(queueId, token)
   182	            if (affected > 0) {
   183	                Timber.i("✅ [Payment Queue] Payment marked as synced | queueId=$queueId")
   184	            } else {
   185	                // 🔒 CAS miss (F-8, Fix round 1): another worker reclaimed this row after our
   186	                // claim went stale. The backend call that got us here already succeeded — this
   187	                // is lost local bookkeeping, not a failed sync. Caller must not retry the write.
   188	                Timber.w("⚠️ [Payment Queue] markSynced() no afectó filas (claim reclamado por otro worker) | queueId=$queueId")
   189	            }
   190	            affected
   191	        } catch (e: CancellationException) {
   192	            // 🔴 F-9, Fix round 2 (pre-existing, not introduced by round 1): CancellationException
   193	            // IS a RuntimeException/Exception in Kotlin — a bare `catch (e: Exception)` below
   194	            // would swallow worker cancellation and return 0 instead of letting it propagate to
   195	            // doWork()'s `catch (e: CancellationException) { throw e }`. A cancelled worker must
   196	            // leave this row claimed for the stale sweep, never keep writing/looping. Rethrow
   197	            // BEFORE the generic catch.
   198	            throw e
   199	        } catch (e: Exception) {
   200	            Timber.e(e, "❌ [Payment Queue] Failed to mark payment as synced")
   201	            0
   202	        }
   203	    }
   204	
   205	    // 🔴 F-9, Fix round 1 (Important 2): NO override fun updateRetry() here on purpose.
   206	    // The repository-level wrapper was deleted — it had zero callers in src/main after F-9
   207	    // removed the in-worker retry loop that used to call it mid-loop (keeping the claim so
   208	    // the SAME worker run could try again). That's structurally gone now: every outcome in
   209	    // PaymentSyncWorker.syncPayment() goes through markSynced (success) or release (anything
   210	    // else), both of which CLEAR the claim. A discoverable, claim-RETAINING write on this
   211	    // interface was exactly the footgun this plan removes — a future caller reaching for "record
   212	    // a failed attempt" here instead of release() would recreate the "claimed row nobody acts on
   213	    // again until stale-claim" bug. The underlying `PendingPaymentDao.updateRetry` Room query
   214	    // still exists (kept for its own CAS regression test, PendingPaymentClaimTest.kt) — it's
   215	    // just no longer exposed through this application-facing repository.
   216	
   217	    override fun observarEstadoDeVenta(referenceNumber: String) = pendingPaymentDao.observarEstadoDeVenta(referenceNumber)
   218

   252	            }

   256	            0

  1218	    /**
  1219	     * Inspects the current cart and produces the payload the navigation layer
  1220	     * needs to open `PaymentScreen`.
  1221	     *
  1222	     * Routing ([caminoDelCobro], D3 del spec «Cobrar sin red»):
  1223	     * - Sólo importes con red → [PaymentNavigationPayload.Fast] (no order is created, mirrors today's "Pago Rápido" flow exactly).
  1224	     * - Con productos y red → la orden completa en `/orders/with-items`, como hoy. La llave (`externalId`) y el carrito quedan en el
  1225	     *   aparato ANTES del POST ([VentaSinRedRepository.guardarCarrito]); si no se pudieron escribir, no se sale a la red.
  1226	     *   Si el servidor no contesta ([pasaALaVentaLocal]) ⇒ venta local con la MISMA llave; otro error (4xx) ⇒ como hoy.
  1227	     * - Sin red (o sólo importes en $0) → venta local: [PaymentNavigationPayload.VentaLocal] (sólo efectivo) o, en $0, la fila ya
  1228	     *   guardada sin abrir la pantalla de cobro ([PaymentNavigationPayload.VentaEnCeroGuardada]).
  1229	     *
  1230	     * Mientras corre, el carrito no cambia ([ventaEnPreparacion]).
  1231	     */

  1234	    private suspend fun prepararCobro(): Result<PaymentNavigationPayload> {
  1235	        val current = _cartState.value
  1236	        errorParaCobrar(current)?.let { return Result.failure(IllegalStateException(it)) }
  1237	        if (current.isEmpty) {
  1238	            return Result.failure(IllegalStateException("Cart is empty"))
  1239	        }
  1240	        if (current.taxCents != 0) {
  1241	            return Result.failure(IllegalStateException("El impuesto no está disponible en Cobrar V1"))
  1242	        }
  1243	        // Un «vaciar» de hace un instante termina de borrar el carrito a medias antes de que esta venta escriba el suyo.
  1244	        borradoDelCarrito?.join()
  1245	
  1246	        val amountPesos = (current.totalCents / 100.0).toString()
  1247	        // 📴 Lo que lleva orden necesita al SERVIDOR: con la franja de «sin servidor» crear la orden esperaba ~25 s y luego caía a la
  1248	        // venta local con la misma llave. Ahora va directo, como sin internet (encargo 97042c). Sólo importes sigue mirando internet:
  1249	        // su cobro rápido no espera al servidor (efectivo a la cola al instante) y la tarjeta puede no necesitarlo (Codex r1 #5).
  1250	        val hayServidor = connectionStateManager.isFullyConnected()
  1251	        // 🔴 D5: el cupón necesita al SERVIDOR: sin red no se guarda como venta local (se subiría como un descuento libre), se dice. Y un
  1252	        // cobro rápido no tiene orden donde ligarlo: con cupón siempre hay orden, aunque sean puros importes. Igual con el descuento del
  1253	        // cliente (D1): se registra como lo que es, en su orden.
  1254	        if (current.couponCode != null && !hayServidor) return Result.failure(IllegalStateException(TEXTO_CUPON_SIN_RED))
  1255	        val llevaOrden = current.hasProductItems || current.couponCode != null || current.descuentoEsDelCliente
  1256	        val hayRed = if (llevaOrden) hayServidor else connectionStateManager.hasInternet()
  1257	        return when (caminoDelCobro(llevaOrden, current.totalCents, hayRed)) {
  1258	            CaminoDelCobro.RAPIDO -> {
  1259	                // Sin orden y sin carrito durable (como hoy). Un carrito a medias de un intento anterior ya no es esta venta.
  1260	                secureStorage.getVenueId()?.let { ventaSinRed.borrarCarrito(it) }
  1261	                // No order is created here, but the referral can still be captured with no `intendedOrderId`. The qualifying-order
  1262	                // webhook on the backend will pick it up via the customerId attached to the fast-payment Order shadow row.
  1263	                capturarReferido(orderId = null)
  1264	                Result.success(PaymentNavigationPayload.Fast(amountPesos))
  1265	            }
  1266	            CaminoDelCobro.EN_LINEA -> cobrarEnLinea(current, amountPesos)
  1267	            CaminoDelCobro.LOCAL -> {
  1268	                // Una venta local se reproduce por /mobile SIN token: una cortesía sin permiso la rechazaría el servidor. El PIN necesita red.
  1269	                if (llevaCortesia(current) && !permissionsRepository.hasPermission("tpv-orders:comp")) {
  1270	                    return Result.failure(IllegalStateException(
  1271	                        (if (hayRed) "" else "Sin conexión: ") + "el PIN del gerente necesita internet. Quita la cortesía o cobra cuando vuelva la red",
  1272	                    ))
  1273	                }
  1274	                // Sólo importes en $0 con red: hasta hoy salía como Fast y capturaba el referido sin orden; eso se conserva.
  1275	                if (hayRed && !current.hasProductItems) capturarReferido(orderId = null)
  1276	                ventaLocal(current, amountPesos, llaveYaUsada = null)
  1277	            }
  1278	        }
  1279	    }
  1280	
  1281	    private suspend fun cobrarEnLinea(current: CartState, amountPesos: String): Result<PaymentNavigationPayload> {
  1282	        val venueId = secureStorage.getVenueId()
  1283	            ?: return Result.failure(IllegalStateException("No venueId in secure storage"))
  1284	        val staffId = staffDe(current) ?: return Result.failure(IllegalStateException("No staffId in secure storage"))
  1285	        val cliente = _selectedCustomer.value
  1286	        val pedido = pedidoEnLinea(current, staffId, cliente?.id)
  1287	        val llave = llaveDelIntento(pedido)
  1288	        // ANTES del POST: si el proceso muere esperando la respuesta, al reabrir Cobrar vuelve este carrito con ESTA llave y el reintento
  1289	        // encuentra la misma orden por `externalId`. Si no quedó escrito, no se sale a la red.
  1290	        if (!ventaSinRed.guardarCarrito(venueId, llave, current, cliente)) {
  1291	            return Result.failure(IllegalStateException(TEXTO_NO_SE_GUARDO_LA_VENTA))
  1292	        }
  1293	        val creada = crearOrdenEnLinea(venueId, pedido, llave)
  1294	        val fallo = creada.exceptionOrNull()
  1295	        if (fallo != null) {
  1296	            if (!pasaALaVentaLocal(fallo)) return Result.failure(fallo) // 4xx, 500…: como hoy (el carrito y su llave se quedan)
  1297	            // D5: el carrito y su llave se quedan: si la creación sí llegó, el reintento encuentra la misma orden.
  1298	            if (current.couponCode != null) return Result.failure(IllegalStateException(TEXTO_CUPON_SIN_RED))
  1299	            Timber.w(fallo, "🛒 El servidor no contestó al crear la orden: sigue como venta local con la misma llave")
  1300	            return ventaLocal(current, amountPesos, llaveYaUsada = llave)
  1301	        }
  1302	        val order = creada.getOrThrow()
  1303	        // La venta en línea terminó de prepararse: la orden ya existe y el cobro sigue en la pantalla de pago.
  1304	        ventaSinRed.borrarCarrito(venueId)
  1305	        // Persist the referral right before we hand off to PaymentScreen. Failures are swallowed: the cart already has the discount
  1306	        // applied, so blocking the charge over a transient backend failure would punish the customer for a backend hiccup.
  1307	        capturarReferido(orderId = order.id)
  1308	        // D5 (Codex r1 #7): con cupón manda el total del SERVIDOR, que acepta un centavo de redondeo: ése se cobra, y sólo es cortesía si
  1309	        // allá quedó en $0 (si no, la cuenta se quedaba debiendo $0.01 con la pantalla diciendo «completada»).
  1310	        val totalDelServidor = order.total.takeIf { current.couponCode != null }
  1311	        val gratis = totalDelServidor?.let { it.signum() == 0 } ?: (current.totalCents == 0)
  1312	        val importe = totalDelServidor?.toPlainString() ?: amountPesos
  1313	        return Result.success(
  1314	            if (gratis) {
  1315	                PaymentNavigationPayload.CompletedFreeCart(
  1316	                    amountPesosString = importe,
  1317	                    orderId = order.id,
  1318	                    orderNumber = order.orderNumber,
  1319	                )
  1320	            } else {
  1321	                PaymentNavigationPayload.Order(
  1322	                    amountPesosString = importe,
  1323	                    orderId = order.id,
  1324	                    orderNumber = order.orderNumber,
  1325	                    wasPayLaterOrder = false,
  1326	                )
  1327	            },
  1328	        )
  1329	    }

  1331	    /**
  1332	     * 💵 La venta local: el pedido para mobile (centavos, `isOfflineReplay`) y los renglones del ticket, con [llaveYaUsada] si ya
  1333	     * hubo un intento en línea (o la del carrito si no). En $0 se guarda YA como fila y no abre la pantalla de cobro; si no, el carrito a
  1334	     * medias se guarda antes de navegar con la venta (sólo efectivo) y la pantalla de cobro inserta la fila (C3).
  1335	     */
  1336	    private suspend fun ventaLocal(current: CartState, amountPesos: String, llaveYaUsada: String?): Result<PaymentNavigationPayload> {
  1337	        // D1 (diseño v5): el descuento del cliente nunca entra a una venta local. Ésta es la única puerta (sin red, y la caída de
  1338	        // `with-items` por red), como la cortesía sin red: se dice y la cajera lo quita o espera la red.
  1339	        if (current.descuentoEsDelCliente) {
  1340	            return Result.failure(IllegalStateException(textoDescuentoSinRed()))
  1341	        }
  1342	        val venueId = secureStorage.getVenueId()
  1343	            ?: return Result.failure(IllegalStateException("No venueId in secure storage"))
  1344	        val staffId = staffDe(current) ?: return Result.failure(IllegalStateException("No staffId in secure storage"))
  1345	        val cliente = _selectedCustomer.value
  1346	        val llave = llaveYaUsada ?: llaveDelIntento(pedidoEnLinea(current, staffId, cliente?.id))
  1347	        if (!ventaSinRed.guardarCarrito(venueId, llave, current, cliente)) {
  1348	            return Result.failure(IllegalStateException(TEXTO_NO_SE_GUARDO_LA_VENTA))
  1349	        }
  1350	        val shiftId = try {
  1351	            ventaSinRed.turnoDeLaVenta(venueId)
  1352	        } catch (e: Exception) {
  1353	            if (e is CancellationException) throw e
  1354	            Timber.e(e, "🛒 No se pudo leer la caja guardada para preparar la venta")
  1355	            return Result.failure(IllegalStateException("No se pudo leer la caja guardada. La venta se conserva; vuelve a intentar.", e))
  1356	        }
  1357	        val productos = _products.value.associateBy { it.id }
  1358	        val ahora = System.currentTimeMillis()
  1359	        val venta = VentaLocal(
  1360	            llave = llave,
  1361	            venueId = venueId,
  1362	            staffId = staffId,
  1363	            shiftId = shiftId,
  1364	            createdAtLocal = ahora,
  1365	            totalCents = current.totalCents,
  1366	            pedidoJson = armarPedidoParaMobile(current, llave, staffId, shiftId, ahora, productos, customerId = cliente?.id),
  1367	            renglones = renglonesDeTicket(current, llave, productos),
  1368	        )
  1369	        if (current.totalCents == 0) {
  1370	            return ventaSinRed.guardarVentaEnCero(venta).fold(
  1371	                onSuccess = {
  1372	                    // El carrito ya terminó: una recreación de la pantalla conserva SU ticket, nunca permite cobrarlo otra vez.
  1373	                    _ventaEnCeroGuardada.value = venta
  1374	                    reiniciarCarrito()
  1375	                    Result.success(PaymentNavigationPayload.VentaEnCeroGuardada(amountPesos, venta))
  1376	                },
  1377	                onFailure = { e ->
  1378	                    Timber.e(e, "🛒 No se pudo guardar la venta en \$0")
  1379	                    Result.failure(if (e is VentaLocalYaGuardadaException) e else IllegalStateException(TEXTO_NO_SE_GUARDO_LA_VENTA, e))
  1380	                },
  1381	            )
  1382	        }
  1383	        return Result.success(PaymentNavigationPayload.VentaLocal(amountPesos, Gson().toJson(venta)))
  1384	    }

  1386	    private suspend fun capturarReferido(orderId: String?) {
  1387	        runCatching { captureReferralOnPayment(orderId = orderId) }
  1388	            .onFailure { e -> Timber.e(e, "🎁 captureReferralOnPayment threw (orderId=$orderId)") }
  1389	    }
  1390	
  1391	    /**
  1392	     * Creates an order with the current cart items linked to the selected
  1393	     * customer, leaving it `paymentStatus = PENDING`. The order shows up in
  1394	     * the dashboard's "Cuentas por Cobrar" view and in the operator's
  1395	     * pay-later list. No PaymentScreen navigation — operator goes back to a
  1396	     * fresh cart after creation.
  1397	     *
  1398	     * Requires `_selectedCustomer != null` (the whole point is binding the
  1399	     * debt to a customer). Cart must have items (no $0 free-cart in this path).
  1400	     * Mientras corre, el carrito no cambia ([ventaEnPreparacion]).
  1401	     *
  1402	     * **Known drift**: when the dashboard later settles this order via the
  1403	     * existing `settleOrder` endpoint, inventory is NOT deducted (the
  1404	     * inventory hook only fires on the regular fully-paid path). Operations
  1405	     * accepts this until `settleOrder` is fixed in a separate ticket. The
  1406	     * order itself is faithful — items, discounts, cortesía all persisted as
  1407	     * with regular Cobrar orders.
  1408	     */
  1409	    suspend fun createPayLaterOrder(): Result<Order> = candado.mientras {
  1410	        val current = _cartState.value
  1411	        val error = if (!connectionStateManager.hasInternet()) {
  1412	            if (current.couponCode != null) TEXTO_CUPON_SIN_RED else "Necesita internet"
  1413	        } else {
  1414	            errorParaCobrar(current)
  1415	        }
  1416	        when {
  1417	            error != null -> Result.failure(IllegalStateException(error))
  1418	            current.isEmpty -> Result.failure(IllegalStateException("El carrito está vacío"))
  1419	            _selectedCustomer.value == null -> Result.failure(IllegalStateException("Selecciona un cliente para Pagar después"))
  1420	            current.totalCents == 0 -> Result.failure(
  1421	                IllegalStateException("El total es \$0 — usa cortesía/cobrar normal en lugar de Pagar después"),
  1422	            )
  1423	            // The backend createOrderWithItems endpoint already accepts customerId
  1424	            // and leaves the order in PENDING + PENDING when total > 0. That's
  1425	            // exactly the pay-later shape. No separate "leave open" flag needed.

   355	     * Used by forceCheck (Reintentar) and network observer.
   356	     * Sends a heartbeat with an 8s timeout for UI responsiveness.
   357	     * Does NOT process pending commands — that's the monitoring loop's job.
   358	     *
   359	     * @param version Anti-stale guard — side effects skipped if superseded
   360	     */
   361	    private suspend fun probeConnectivity(version: Int) {
   362	        if (!deviceInfoManager.isDeviceActivated()) {
   363	            if (isLatest(version)) _state.value = ConnectionState.Connected
   364	            return
   365	        }
   366	
   367	        val networkInfo = networkMonitor.getCurrentNetworkInfo()
   368	        if (!networkInfo.isConnected) {
   369	            if (isLatest(version)) {
   370	                scheduleOfflineTransition("probe")
   371	            }
   372	            return
   373	        }
   374	
   375	        try {
   376	            Timber.d("🌐 [Connection] Probe: checking backend connectivity...")
   377	
   378	            if (isLatest(version)) {
   379	                val wasDisconnected = _state.value is ConnectionState.DisconnectedNoInternet ||
   380	                        _state.value is ConnectionState.DisconnectedServerDown
   381	                if (wasDisconnected) _state.value = ConnectionState.Reconnecting
   382	            }
   383	
   384	            val heartbeat = buildLightweightHeartbeat()
   385	            val startMs = System.currentTimeMillis()
   386	
   387	            // Short timeout for UI responsiveness — don't wait the full 30s OkHttp timeout
   388	            val result = withTimeout(8_000) {
   389	                heartbeatRepository.sendHeartbeat(heartbeat)
   390	            }
   391	            val latencyMs = System.currentTimeMillis() - startMs
   392	
   393	            // After suspension point — re-check version before writing ANY side effect
   394	            if (!isLatest(version)) return
   395	
   396	            when (result) {
   397	                is Result.Success -> {
   398	                    Timber.i("✅ [Connection] Probe OK (latency=${latencyMs}ms)")
   399	                    cancelOfflineTransition()
   400	                    markServerProbeSucceeded()
   401	                    connectionStateManager.updateState(hasInternet = true, hasServer = true, latencyMs = latencyMs)
   402	                    handleReconnectionSuccess(version)
   403	
   404	                    // Process pending commands — critical for offline→online transition
   405	                    // Backend marks commands as SENT when included in ANY heartbeat response,
   406	                    // so we must process them here or they'll be lost (never re-delivered)
   407	                    val pendingCommands = result.data.pendingCommands
   408	                    if (!pendingCommands.isNullOrEmpty()) {
   409	                        Timber.i("📥 [Connection] Probe received ${pendingCommands.size} pending command(s)")
   410	                        processPendingCommands(pendingCommands)
   411	                    }
   412	                }
   413	                is Result.Error -> {
   414	                    Timber.w("⚠️ [Connection] Probe: backend unreachable: ${result.exception?.message}")
   415	                    markServerProbeFailed(source = "probe", cause = result.exception)
   416	                }
   417	            }
   418	        } catch (e: TimeoutCancellationException) {
   419	            if (!isLatest(version)) return
   420	            Timber.w("⚠️ [Connection] Probe timed out (8s)")
   421	            markServerProbeFailed(source = "probe_timeout", cause = e)
   422	        } catch (e: CancellationException) {
   423	            throw e  // Coroutine cancelled — rethrow, don't set error state
   424	        } catch (e: Exception) {
   425	            if (!isLatest(version)) return
   426	            Timber.e(e, "❌ [Connection] Probe failed")
   427	            markServerProbeFailed(source = "probe_exception", cause = e)
   428	        }
   429	    }
   430	
   431	    /**
   432	     * Full heartbeat with command processing
   433	     *
   434	     * Used ONLY by the monitoring loop. Sends heartbeat (full OkHttp timeout),
   435	     * updates UI state, AND processes pending commands from the response.
   436	     *
   437	     * @param version Anti-stale guard — UI side effects skipped if superseded.
   438	     *                Commands ALWAYS execute regardless of staleness.
   439	     */
   440	    private suspend fun performFullHeartbeat(version: Int) {
   441	        if (!deviceInfoManager.isDeviceActivated()) {
   442	            if (isLatest(version)) _state.value = ConnectionState.Connected
   443	            return
   444	        }
   445	
   446	        val networkInfo = networkMonitor.getCurrentNetworkInfo()
   447	        if (!networkInfo.isConnected) {
   448	            if (isLatest(version)) {
   449	                scheduleOfflineTransition("heartbeat")
   450	            }
   451	            return
   452	        }
   453	
   454	        try {
   455	            Timber.d("🌐 [Connection] Heartbeat: checking backend connectivity...")
   456	
   457	            if (isLatest(version)) {
   458	                if (_state.value is ConnectionState.DisconnectedNoInternet || _state.value is ConnectionState.DisconnectedServerDown) {
   459	                    _state.value = ConnectionState.Reconnecting
   460	                }
   461	            }
   462	
   463	            val heartbeat = buildLightweightHeartbeat()
   464	            val heartbeatStartMs = System.currentTimeMillis()
   465	            val result = heartbeatRepository.sendHeartbeat(heartbeat)
   466	            val latencyMs = System.currentTimeMillis() - heartbeatStartMs
   467	
   468	            when (result) {
   469	                is Result.Success -> {
   470	                    Timber.i("✅ [Connection] Heartbeat OK (latency=${latencyMs}ms)")
   471	
   472	                    // UI state — gated by version
   473	                    if (isLatest(version)) {
   474	                        cancelOfflineTransition()
   475	                        markServerProbeSucceeded()
   476	                        connectionStateManager.updateState(hasInternet = true, hasServer = true, latencyMs = latencyMs)
   477	                        handleReconnectionSuccess(version)
   478	                    }
   479	
   480	                    // Commands — ALWAYS processed, never skipped by stale guard
   481	                    val pendingCommands = result.data.pendingCommands
   482	                    if (!pendingCommands.isNullOrEmpty()) {
   483	                        Timber.i("📥 [Connection] Received ${pendingCommands.size} pending command(s)")
   484	                        processPendingCommands(pendingCommands)
   485	                    }
   486	                }
   487	                is Result.Error -> {
   488	                    Timber.w("⚠️ [Connection] Heartbeat: backend unreachable: ${result.exception?.message}")
   489	                    if (isLatest(version)) {
   490	                        markServerProbeFailed(source = "heartbeat", cause = result.exception)
   491	                    }
   492	                }
   493	            }
   494	        } catch (e: CancellationException) {
   495	            throw e
   496	        } catch (e: Exception) {
   497	            Timber.e(e, "❌ [Connection] Heartbeat failed")
   498	            if (isLatest(version)) {
   499	                markServerProbeFailed(source = "heartbeat_exception", cause = e)
   500	            }
   501	        }
   502	    }
   503	
   504	    /**
   505	     * Mark a server probe as failed.
   506	     *
   507	     * 🛡️ Centralizes the "we couldn't reach the backend" handling for both
   508	     * [probeConnectivity] and [performFullHeartbeat]. Implements the hysteresis
   509	     * rule (SERVER_DOWN_FAILURE_THRESHOLD) so a single transient stall doesn't
   510	     * flicker the banner, plus single-shot Crashlytics non-fatal so each real
   511	     * incident gets exactly one event in the dashboard.
   512	     *
   513	     * @param source Logging tag — "probe", "probe_timeout", "probe_exception",
   514	     *               "heartbeat", "heartbeat_exception". Becomes a Crashlytics key.
   515	     * @param cause Exception that triggered the failure (if any). Attached to the
   516	     *              non-fatal so we can see the actual stack (e.g. Http2Stream timeout).
   517	     */
   518	    private fun markServerProbeFailed(source: String, cause: Throwable? = null) {
   519	        ultimaCausaFallo = generateSequence(cause) { it.cause }.lastOrNull()?.javaClass?.simpleName ?: source
   520	        consecutiveServerProbeFailures++
   521	        val gap = System.currentTimeMillis() - lastSuccessfulProbeAt
   522	
   523	        Timber.w(
   524	            "⚠️ [Connection] Server probe failed ($source) — streak=$consecutiveServerProbeFailures, gap=${gap}ms"
   525	        )
   526	        CrashlyticsContext.logNetworkBreadcrumb(
   527	            "probe failed: source=$source streak=$consecutiveServerProbeFailures gap=${gap}ms"
   528	        )
   529	
   530	        // Below threshold → keep showing Connected/banner, give the network a
   531	        // chance to recover before painting the red banner. This filters out
   532	        // single-failure noise from HTTP/2 stale streams that pingInterval will
   533	        // evict within 15s.
   534	        if (consecutiveServerProbeFailures < SERVER_DOWN_FAILURE_THRESHOLD) {
   535	            // 🛡️ If the UI is currently on a transient state (Reconnecting from
   536	            // forceCheck / post-disconnect probe, or Checking from app init), it
   537	            // would otherwise stay stuck on the spinner until the next monitoring
   538	            // loop iteration (5-30s). Revert to the last confirmed terminal state
   539	            // so the user sees a definitive UI.
   540	            if (_state.value is ConnectionState.Reconnecting ||
   541	                _state.value is ConnectionState.Checking
   542	            ) {
   543	                _state.value = lastConfirmedConnectionState
   544	            }
   545	            // Schedule a fast follow-up probe so we hit SERVER_DOWN_FAILURE_THRESHOLD
   546	            // within ~2s instead of waiting the regular 5-30s monitoring backoff.
   547	            // Critical for forceCheck UX: without this, "Reintentar" gives no
   548	            // definitive feedback for up to 30s after a single failed probe.
   549	            scheduleFastReprobe(reason = source)
   550	            return
   551	        }
   552	
   553	        // At/over threshold → declare server-down (banner shows)
   554	        fastReprobeJob?.cancel()
   555	        fastReprobeJob = null
   556	        cancelOfflineTransition() // Internet confirmed — cancel pending NoInternet
   557	        if (!isDismissed) _state.value = ConnectionState.DisconnectedServerDown
   558	        lastConfirmedConnectionState = ConnectionState.DisconnectedServerDown
   559	        connectionStateManager.updateState(hasInternet = true, hasServer = false)
   560	        reconnectionAttempts++
   561	
   562	        // Single-shot non-fatal per incident (cooldown UNREACHABLE_NONFATAL_COOLDOWN_MS)
   563	        // First time: lastUnreachableNonFatalAt == 0 → fires immediately
   564	        // Repeat firings only after cooldown to avoid spam during a long outage
   565	        val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
   566	        if (now - lastUnreachableNonFatalAt > UNREACHABLE_NONFATAL_COOLDOWN_MS) {
   567	            CrashlyticsContext.recordBackendUnreachable(
   568	                gapSinceLastSuccessMs = gap,
   569	                cause = cause,
   570	                source = source,

   816	     * **Behavior (2) — Sync-on-reconnect:**
   817	     * Triggers an immediate offline-queue sync so queued payments record in seconds
   818	     * instead of waiting up to 15 minutes for the periodic WorkManager job.

   820	    private fun listenToConnectionRestored() {
   821	        viewModelScope.launch {
   822	            connectionEventManager.connectionRestoredEvents.collect { event ->
   823	                Timber.i("🔄 [PaymentViewModel] Connection restored after ${event.attemptsBeforeReconnection} attempts")
   824	
   825	                // Behavior (2): Trigger immediate offline-queue sync
   826	                PaymentSyncScheduler.runNow(appContext)
   827	                Timber.i("🔄 [PaymentViewModel] Immediate offline-queue sync requested")
   828	
   829	                // Behavior (1): Re-attempt backend recording if success screen shows blank QR
   830	                val retry = pendingReceiptRetry
   831	                // na16c21f: también con «No se pudo imprimir» abierto (su éxito guardado sin recibo).
   832	                val exitoSinRecibo = exitoVisible(_state.value)?.takeIf { it.receipt == null }
   833	                if (retry != null && exitoSinRecibo != null) {
   834	                    Timber.i("🔄 [PaymentViewModel] Re-attempting backend recording for pending receipt | ref=${retry.referenceNumber}")
   835	                    try {
   836	                        // 🔴 NonCancellable: si el proceso muere/la pantalla se abandona a
   837	                        // media espera (recordPaymentUseCase reintenta hasta 5 veces con
   838	                        // backoff, ~67.5s peor caso), la llamada NO debe abortarse a medio
   839	                        // camino — sin esto el cobro puede saltarse la cola offline por
   840	                        // completo: dinero cobrado, sin registro, sin fila en la cola.
   841	                        val result = withContext(NonCancellable) {
   842	                            recordPaymentUseCase(
   843	                                context = retry.context,
   844	                                cardDetails = retry.cardDetails,
   845	                                authorizationNumber = retry.authorizationNumber,
   846	                                referenceNumber = retry.referenceNumber,
   847	                            )
   848	                        }
   849	                        result.onSuccess { receipt ->
   850	                            Timber.i("✅ [PaymentViewModel] Receipt recovered on reconnect | paymentId=${receipt.paymentId}")
   851	                            applyRecordedReceiptToSuccessState(
   852	                                receipt = receipt,
   853	                                cardDetails = retry.cardDetails,
   854	                                referenceNumber = retry.referenceNumber,
   855	                                orderIdForFlow = retry.orderIdForFlow,
   856	                                llave = retry.context.idempotencyKey,

   859	                            if (pendingReceiptRetry === retry) pendingReceiptRetry = null
   860	                        }.onFailure { error ->
   861	                            Timber.w("⚠️ [PaymentViewModel] Receipt retry failed on reconnect (will retry next reconnect): ${error.message}")
   862	                            // Leave pendingReceiptRetry intact — will retry on next reconnect event
   863	                            // The periodic queue worker is the backstop
   864	                        }
   865	                    } catch (e: CancellationException) {
   866	                        // CancellationException es RuntimeException en Kotlin: relanzar ANTES
   867	                        // del catch(Exception) general, o se traga y rompe la cancelacion
   868	                        // estructurada.
   869	                        throw e
   870	                    } catch (e: Exception) {
   871	                        Timber.e(e, "❌ [PaymentViewModel] Unexpected error during receipt retry on reconnect")
   872	                    }
   873	                }
   874	            }
   875	        }

  1360	            llave = llave,
  1361	            venueId = venueId,
  1362	            staffId = staffId,

  1380	                },
  1381	            )
  1382	        }
  1383	        return Result.success(PaymentNavigationPayload.VentaLocal(amountPesos, Gson().toJson(venta)))
  1384	    }

  1395	     * pay-later list. No PaymentScreen navigation — operator goes back to a
  1396	     * fresh cart after creation.

  1400	     * Mientras corre, el carrito no cambia ([ventaEnPreparacion]).

  1404	     * inventory hook only fires on the regular fully-paid path). Operations

    69	    override suspend fun carritoParaRestaurar(venueId: String): CarritoAMedias? {
    70	        val guardado = carritoEnCurso.leerGuardado(venueId) ?: return null
    71	        val hayFila = try {
    72	            dao.existeFilaConLlave(guardado.llave)
    73	        } catch (e: Exception) {
    74	            if (e is CancellationException) throw e
    75	            // Sin poder confirmarlo, restaurar podría duplicar una venta ya guardada: no se restaura, y se conserva para la próxima.
    76	            Timber.e(e, "🛒 [VentaSinRed] No se pudo revisar si la venta a medias ya tiene fila; no se restaura")
    77	            throw IllegalStateException("No se pudo comprobar si la venta ya quedó cobrada. Se conserva; vuelve a intentar.", e)
    78	        }
    79	        val decision = queHacerConElCarritoAMedias(guardado.guardadoEn, System.currentTimeMillis(), hayFila)
    80	        if (decision == QueHacerConElCarritoAMedias.RESTAURAR && !guardado.cart.isEmpty) {
    81	            return CarritoAMedias(guardado.llave, guardado.cart, guardado.cliente)
    82	        }
    83	        Timber.i("🛒 [VentaSinRed] Carrito a medias descartado (fila=$hayFila, guardadoEn=${guardado.guardadoEn}, vacío=${guardado.cart.isEmpty})")
    84	        carritoEnCurso.borrar(venueId)
    85	        return null
    86	    }

    74	     * **Constraints:**
    75	     * - Requires network connection (CONNECTED)
    76	     * - No battery/charging constraints (payments must sync ASAP)
    77	     *
    78	     * **Important:** Using KEEP policy prevents restarting worker on every login.
    79	     * This preserves WorkManager's exponential backoff state if worker is retrying.
    80	     *
    81	     * @param context Application or Activity context
    82	     */
    83	    fun start(context: Context) {
    84	        Timber.d("🚀 Starting payment sync scheduler (interval: ${PAYMENT_SYNC_INTERVAL_MINUTES}min)")
    85	
    86	        // Define constraints: Only run when network is available
    87	        val constraints = Constraints.Builder()
    88	            .setRequiredNetworkType(NetworkType.CONNECTED) // Don't run when offline
    89	            .build()
    90	
    91	        // Build periodic work request
    92	        val paymentSyncWorkRequest = PeriodicWorkRequestBuilder<PaymentSyncWorker>(
    93	            repeatInterval = PAYMENT_SYNC_INTERVAL_MINUTES,
    94	            repeatIntervalTimeUnit = TimeUnit.MINUTES
    95	        )
    96	            .setConstraints(constraints)
    97	            .addTag(PAYMENT_SYNC_WORK_NAME)
    98	            .build()
    99	
   100	        // Enqueue work (KEEP policy preserves existing worker)
   101	        WorkManager.getInstance(context).enqueueUniquePeriodicWork(
   102	            PAYMENT_SYNC_WORK_NAME,
   103	            ExistingPeriodicWorkPolicy.KEEP, // Keep existing work if already running
   104	            paymentSyncWorkRequest
   105	        )
   153	     */
   154	    suspend fun isRunning(context: Context): Boolean {
   155	        val workInfos = WorkManager.getInstance(context)
   156	            .getWorkInfosForUniqueWork(PAYMENT_SYNC_WORK_NAME)
   157	            .get()
   158	
   159	        val isRunning = workInfos.isNotEmpty() && workInfos.any { workInfo ->
   160	            !workInfo.state.isFinished
   161	        }
   162	
   163	        Timber.d("Payment sync running: $isRunning")
   164	        return isRunning
   165	    }
   166	
   167	    /**
   168	     * Force immediate sync execution (for testing and manual retry)
   169	     *
   170	     * **Use cases:**
   171	     * - Debugging: Test sync logic immediately
   172	     * - User action: "Sync Now" button in UI
   173	     * - After queueing payment: Trigger immediate sync attempt
   174	     *
   175	     * WorkManager doesn't guarantee immediate execution,
   176	     * but this requests it to run ASAP (usually within 10 seconds).
   177	     *
   178	     * **Important:** This creates a ONE-TIME work request (doesn't affect periodic worker)
   179	     *
   180	     * @param context Application or Activity context
   181	     */
   182	    fun runNow(context: Context) {
   183	        Timber.d("🔥 Requesting immediate payment sync execution")
   184	
   185	        val constraints = Constraints.Builder()
   186	            .setRequiredNetworkType(NetworkType.CONNECTED)
   187	            .build()
   188	
   189	        val immediateWorkRequest = androidx.work.OneTimeWorkRequestBuilder<PaymentSyncWorker>()
   190	            .setConstraints(constraints)
   191	            .build()
   192	
   193	        // KEEP, no REPLACE: si ya hay un worker drenando la cola queremos que TERMINE.
   194	        // REPLACE lo mataria a media tanda y las filas reclamadas tendrian que esperar
   195	        // al umbral de stale-claim para volver a salir.
   196	        WorkManager.getInstance(context).enqueueUniqueWork(
   197	            IMMEDIATE_WORK_NAME,
   198	            androidx.work.ExistingWorkPolicy.KEEP,
   199	            immediateWorkRequest,
   200	        )
   201	
   202	        Timber.d("✅ Immediate payment sync requested")
   203	    }

   212	     * **Note:** This requires injecting PaymentQueueRepository.

   214	     *
   215	     * Alternative: Use LiveData/Flow from repository in ViewModel layer.

   217	    // TODO: Implement getPendingCount() using repository (requires DI)

   623	     *
   624	     * Shared by probeConnectivity and performFullHeartbeat.
   625	     * Cancels any previous banner timer to avoid duplicates.
   626	     *
   627	     * @param version Anti-stale guard
   628	     */
   629	    private suspend fun handleReconnectionSuccess(version: Int) {
   630	        if (!isLatest(version)) return
   631	
   632	        if (reconnectionAttempts > 0) {
   633	            viewModelScope.launch { commandExecutor.recoverPending() }
   634	            // Refresh paid access after recovery without delaying the connection banner or checkout.
   635	            viewModelScope.launch {
   636	                tpvSettingsRepository.refreshFromTerminalConfig(deviceInfoManager.getSerialNumber())
   637	            }
   638	            Timber.i("🔄 [Connection] Connection restored after $reconnectionAttempts attempts")
   639	
   640	            connectionEventManager.emitConnectionRestored(
   641	                attemptsBeforeReconnection = reconnectionAttempts
   642	            )
   643	
   644	            // Re-check after suspension point (emitConnectionRestored may suspend)
   645	            if (!isLatest(version)) return
   646	
   647	            _state.value = ConnectionState.Reconnected
   648	
   649	            // Cancel previous banner timer to avoid duplicate transitions
   650	            reconnectedBannerJob?.cancel()
   651	            reconnectedBannerJob = viewModelScope.launch {
   652	                delay(2000)
   653	                if (_state.value is ConnectionState.Reconnected) {
   654	                    _state.value = ConnectionState.Connected
   655	                }
   656	            }
   657	
   658	            reconnectionAttempts = 0
   659	        } else {
   660	            _state.value = ConnectionState.Connected
   661	        }
   662	    }
   663	
   664	    /**
   665	     * Calculate exponential backoff delay
   666	     *
   667	     * Pattern (Toast POS):
   668	     * - 1st attempt: 5s
   669	     * - 2nd attempt: 10s
   670	     * - 3rd attempt: 20s
   671	     * - 4th attempt: 30s
   672	     * - 5th+ attempt: 30s (max)
   673	     */
   674	    private fun calculateBackoffDelay(): Long {
   675	        val baseDelay = 5_000L // 5 seconds
   676	        val maxDelay = 30_000L // 30 seconds
   677	
   678	        val delay = (baseDelay * (1 shl minOf(reconnectionAttempts, 4))).coerceAtMost(maxDelay)
   679	        Timber.d("⏱️ [Connection] Retry in ${delay / 1000}s (attempt #${reconnectionAttempts + 1})")
   680	        return delay
   681	    }
   682	
   683	    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   684	    // WiFi sin salida (29-sep-2026): ver `ControlDeWifi` y `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-29-wifi-sin-salida-autorrecuperacion.md`
   506	     *
   507	     * **Design Pattern (Square/Toast):**
   508	     * Start heartbeat + config fetch AFTER all permission dialogs are resolved.
   509	     * This prevents init from causing UI recomposition that dismisses permission dialogs.
   510	     * Called from: cameraLocationPermissionLauncher callback OR requestCameraLocationPermissions
   511	     * when permissions are already granted.
   512	     */
   513	    private fun startPostPermissionInitialization() {
   514	        lifecycleScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
   515	            startHeartbeatIfActivated()
   516	            fetchTerminalConfigIfActivated()
   517	        }
   518	    }
   519	
   520	    /**
   521	     * - Heartbeat runs whenever app is open AND terminal is activated
   522	     * - Does NOT depend on login state
   523	     * - Enables backend to track terminal online/offline status
   524	     * - User can still login even if terminal was marked INACTIVE
   525	     *
   526	     * **Why here instead of in login?**
   527	     * - Prevents deadlock: Terminal can be INACTIVE after logout, but login should still work
   528	     * - Matches industry pattern: Square/Toast start heartbeat on app launch, not login
   529	     * - Allows backend visibility into terminals even when no user is logged in
   530	     */
   531	    private fun startHeartbeatIfActivated() {
   532	        val isActivated = secureStorage.isTerminalActivated()
   533	
   534	        if (isActivated) {
   535	            Timber.d("🟢 Terminal is activated - starting heartbeat scheduler")
   536	            HeartbeatScheduler.start(applicationContext)
   537	            // El kiosco restaurado evita Splash; ambos modos pasan por este arranque.
   538	            if (secureStorage.isAuthenticated() && !secureStorage.getStaffId().isNullOrBlank() &&
   539	                !secureStorage.getVenueId().isNullOrBlank()) {
   540	                try {
   541	                    PaymentSyncScheduler.start(applicationContext)
   542	                } catch (cancelled: kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException) {
   543	                    throw cancelled
   544	                } catch (error: Exception) {
   545	                    Timber.e(error, "No se pudo agendar la recuperación de la cola guardada")
   546	                }
   547	            }
   548	        } else {
   549	            Timber.d("🔴 Terminal not activated - heartbeat will not start until activation")
   550	        }
   551	    }
   552	
   553	    /**
   554	     * Fetch terminal configuration from backend if activated
   555	     *
   556	     * **Phase 2: Dynamic Multi-Merchant Configuration**
   557	     * - Fetches merchant accounts assigned to this terminal by superadmin
   558	     * - Replaces hardcoded SANDBOX_ACCOUNT_A/B with backend-managed config
   559	     * - Enables multi-merchant routing (Account A for main restaurant, Account B for bar)
   560	     *
   561	     * **Design Pattern (Square/Toast):**
   562	     * - Configuration loaded on app startup (NOT on login)
   563	     * - Updates MerchantRepository with fetched merchants
   564	     * - Allows PaymentViewModel to show merchant selection dialog
   565	     *
   566	     * **Flow:**
   567	     * 1. Get device serial number (e.g., "AVQD-2841548417")
   568	     * 2. Call GET /tpv/terminals/:serial/config
   569	     * 3. Update MerchantRepository with fetched merchants
   570	     * 4. PaymentViewModel reactively updates via getMerchantsUseCase
   571	     *
   572	     * **Error Handling:**
   573	     * - Silently fails with log warning (doesn't block app startup)
   574	     * - Falls back to hardcoded sandbox accounts if backend unreachable
   575	     * - User can still process payments with fallback accounts
   576	     */
   577	    private suspend fun fetchTerminalConfigIfActivated() {
   578	        // Runs on Dispatchers.IO (called from onCreate)
   579	        var isActivated = secureStorage.isTerminalActivated()
   580	
   581	        // 🐛 FIX: On fresh install, local cache is empty but terminal may still be activated.
   582	        // Check backend activation status to handle reinstall/cache clear scenarios.
   583	        if (!isActivated) {

   879	    /**
   880	     * Applies the referral percent discount as a manual order-level discount
   881	     * tagged with [REFERRAL_DISCOUNT_SOURCE]. Computed against the current
   882	     * gross subtotal (matching what `ManualDiscountSubView` does for PERCENT
   883	     * mode).
   884	     */
   885	    private fun applyReferralDiscount(discountPercent: Int, referrerName: String) {
   886	        val pct = discountPercent.coerceIn(0, 100)
   887	        if (pct == 0) return
   888	        val subtotal = _cartState.value.subtotalCents
   889	        if (subtotal <= 0) return
   890	        val amount = (subtotal * pct / 100).coerceAtMost(subtotal)
   891	        applyManualOrderDiscountInternal(
   892	            amountCents = amount,
   893	            reason = "Referido por $referrerName ($pct%)",
   894	            source = REFERRAL_DISCOUNT_SOURCE,
   895	        )
   896	    }
   897	
   898	    /**
   899	     * Persists the PENDING Referral row right before the payment is
   900	     * initiated. Idempotent: no-op when the validation state isn't Valid.
   901	     *
   902	     * Returns true on success (or no-op), false if the backend rejected
   903	     * persistence. The caller can decide whether to block payment on a
   904	     * failed capture; the recommendation is NOT to block (record locally
   905	     * and let the user finish the charge), since the discount was already
   906	     * applied to the cart.
   907	     */
   908	    suspend fun captureReferralOnPayment(orderId: String? = null): Boolean {
   909	        if (!connectionStateManager.hasInternet()) return false
   910	        val state = _referralValidation.value
   911	        if (state !is ReferralCaptureUiState.Valid) return true
   912	        val venueId = secureStorage.getVenueId() ?: return false
   913	        val customer = _selectedCustomer.value ?: return false
   914	        val staffVenueId = secureStorage.getStaffId() ?: return false
   915	        val code = _referralCode.value.trim()
   916	        if (code.isEmpty()) return false
   917	
   918	        val result = captureReferralUseCase(
   919	            venueId = venueId,
   920	            referralCode = code,
   921	            newCustomerId = customer.id,
   922	            capturedByStaffVenueId = staffVenueId,
   923	            intendedOrderId = orderId,
   924	        )
   925	        result.onFailure { e ->
   926	            // If the validation reason changed between validate + capture,
   927	            // surface it in the UI so the next attempt sees the new reason.
   928	            (e as? ReferralValidationException)?.let { ex ->
   929	                _referralValidation.value = ReferralCaptureUiState.Invalid(ex.reason)
   930	                clearReferralDiscountOnly()
   931	            }
   932	            Timber.e(e, "🎁 captureReferralOnPayment failed")
   933	        }
   934	        return result.isSuccess
   935	    }

   202	    suspend operator fun invoke(
   203	        context: PaymentContext,
